Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The computer_vision variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 808 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 151 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on computer_vision structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 808. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 151. QED.
